Invoke to convert a PNG to a transparent SVG, vectorise an AI-generated image or logo, or fix a checkerboard background; also German. Don't use for PNGs that already carry real alpha (vectorise directly), or for photographic content where the background isn't a flat fake-transparency pattern.
